When used as a subroutine in SCP, the learned approach delivers over a 30$\times$ speedup compared to COSMO.

</details>


### [100] [Delay-dependent passivity and stability of linear port-Hamiltonian systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13897)
*Ikram El Haskouki,Hannes Gernandt*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了含时滞的线性端口-哈密顿系统的时滞相关与无关的无源性特性，利用特定存储函数和Wirtinger不等式推导了无源性条件，并回顾了时滞相关与无关的指数稳定性条件，最后将无源性和稳定性结果应用于区域供热网络和含时滞阻尼/刚度的振荡器模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时滞现象在工程系统中普遍存在，特别是端口-哈密顿系统（一种重要的非线性系统建模框架）在引入时滞后的无源性和稳定性分析尚未充分研究，本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 采用特定存储函数（Lyapunov函数）结合Wirtinger不等式推导时滞相关与无关的无源性条件，同时利用现有稳定性理论给出时滞相关/无关的指数稳定性判据。

Result: 获得了线性端口-哈密顿系统在时滞影响下的无源性充分条件（包括时滞相关和时滞无关两种情形），以及相应的指数稳定性条件。

Conclusion: 所提出的无源性和稳定性条件可成功应用于区域供热网络和含时滞阻尼/刚度的振荡器等实际工程系统，验证了理论结果的有效性。

Abstract: We study delay-dependent and independent passivity properties of linear port-Hamiltonian systems with delay terms. We derive passivity conditions using a particular storage function and Wirtinger-based inequalities. Furthermore, we recall several conditions for delay-dependent and independent exponential stability. Finally, we present an application of our passivity and stability results to delay equations arising in the modeling of district heating networks and oscillators with delayed damping and stiffness.

</details>


### [101] [Proof and More Variations of Bellman's Lost-in-a-forest Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13987)
*Zhipeng Deng*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文在前期工作的基础上，给出了Bellman"迷失森林"问题的通解证明，并将该问题与曲线覆盖、带邻域旅行商问题建立了形式化联系，证明了等价性与收敛性，同时探讨了双线搜索、Wetzel单位弧覆盖、封闭路径及三维空间等变体，提供了通用计算公式、部分解析结果及数值结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在前期对Bellman迷失森林问题的一般化公式与计算方法的基础上，进一步完善通解证明，并将该问题与曲线覆盖问题和带邻域的旅行商问题建立统一的理论框架。

Method: 采用形式化方法将迷失森林问题、曲线覆盖问题与带邻域TSP问题统一建模，证明三者之间的等价性与收敛性；通过对双线搜索、Wetzel单位弧覆盖、封闭路径变体、三维空间变体等场景的扩展，推导通用计算公式，并结合解析与数值计算给出结果。

Result: 获得了迷失森林问题的通解证明；建立了与曲线覆盖、带邻域TSP问题的等价性关系；得到了双线搜索、Wetzel问题、封闭路径和三维变体下的通用计算公式、部分解析结果及数值结果。

Conclusion: 迷失森林问题与曲线覆盖、带邻域TSP问题在形式化框架下等价且收敛，通过扩展变体研究获得了更广泛的计算结果，为该类路径优化问题提供了统一的理论与方法支持。

Abstract: In this paper, based on our previous general formulation and computational solution to Bellman's Lost-in-a-forest Problem, we provide the proof of general solution and obtained more variations and results related to this problem. This paper provides generalized formalized method connecting curve covering, lost-in-the-forest problem, and traveling salesman problem with neighborhoods. We prove the equivalence and convergence. We also provide more results of searching for two lines, connection to Wetzel's unit arc covering problem, variations with closed path, variations in three dimensions, etc. The results include general calculation equations, partial analytical results, and numerical results.

</details>


### [102] [A Semismooth Newton-Based Proximal Augmented Lagrangian Method for Joint Estimation of Multiple Gaussian Graphical Models with Clustered Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14041)
*Han Wang,Yue Liu,Yong-Jin Liu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对聚类多高斯图模型联合估计中的凸复合优化问题，提出了一种结合对数行列式损失与稀疏聚类正则子的模型，建立了解为块对角化的充要条件以实现问题分解，并开发了基于近端增广拉格朗日方法与半光滑牛顿法的高效求解算法，在合成与真实数据上验证了其优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决多高斯图模型联合估计中，如何高效且鲁棒地同时鼓励不同图之间的稀疏模式相似性和边值一致性这一优化问题，克服一阶方法收敛慢、鲁棒性不足的缺陷。

Method: 提出近端增广拉格朗日方法（PALM），每个子问题采用超线性收敛的半光滑牛顿法求解，充分挖掘二阶信息；同时给出解的块对角化充要条件，将大规模问题分解为独立子问题。

Result: 在合成数据和真实数据上，所提算法在收敛速度和鲁棒性方面均优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的二阶方法能有效解决聚类多高斯图模型的联合估计问题，兼具高效率和强鲁棒性。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider a class of convex composite optimization problems arising from the joint estimation of clustered multiple Gaussian graphical models. The resulting model combines a log-determinant loss term with a nonsmooth sparse clustered regularizer, which encourages both similar sparsity patterns and consistent edge values across different graphs. We first establish a necessary and sufficient condition under which the solution is block diagonal, enabling a large-scale problem to be decomposed into smaller independent subproblems and substantially reducing computational complexity. To efficiently solve this problem, we develop a proximal augmented Lagrangian method in which each subproblem is handled by a superlinearly convergent semismooth Newton method. Unlike widely used first order methods, our approach fully exploits the underlying second order information through the semismooth Newton framework, thereby achieving faster convergence and improved robustness. The efficiency and robustness of the proposed algorithm are demonstrated through comparisons with state-of-the-art methods on both synthetic and real data sets.

</details>


### [103] [Battery Bidding under Price Uncertainty in Wholesale Electricity Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14050)
*Vincent Yinjun-Wang,Madeleine Udell*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文构建了价格接受型电池储能在日前市场中的报价模型，发现即使没有市场势力，储能稀缺和价格不确定性也会导致策略性 withholding 行为；不确定性对报价的影响取决于荷电状态；风险管理使报价曲线呈现分层结构以平衡利润与风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电网规模电池在批发电力市场中的影响日益增大，但其报价模式难以解释——特别是看似策略性 withholding 的报价可能源于价格不确定性和风险管理下的理性运营，需要从方法论上区分市场势力行为与理性运营行为。

Method: 建立价格接受型电池资产级模型，在有限价格场景下于日前市场提交阶梯式买卖报价曲线，以均值-CVaR最大化为目标，受物理约束和市场约束。原始问题为混合整数线性规划，但通过整数决策消除得到精确线性规划重构。

Result: 三类核心发现：(1) 即使无市场势力，储量稀缺和价格不确定性也会导致 withholding；(2) 不确定性对报价影响方向取决于荷电状态——稀缺时提高卖价，充裕时降低卖价；(3) 风险管理使报价曲线分层，在保障多情景盈利的同时保留对罕见高价事件的暴露。

Conclusion: 电池储能的 withholding 行为不能简单归因于市场势力，价格不确定性和风险管理是同等重要的解释因素；提出了可用于实证分析的线性规划框架。

Abstract: Grid-scale batteries increasingly influence outcomes in wholesale electricity markets, but their observed bid patterns remain difficult to interpret. In particular, bids that appear to reflect strategic withholding may instead arise from rational operations under price uncertainty and risk management. We develop an asset-level model of a price-taking battery that submits stepwise buy and sell bid curves in the day-ahead market under a finite set of price scenarios. The battery chooses quantity--price pairs to maximize a mean--CVaR objective subject to physical and market constraints. A direct formulation is a mixed-integer linear program, but we show that its integer decisions can be removed, yielding an exact linear programming reformulation suitable for empirical analysis. Our empirical results deliver three insights. First, withholding behavior can arise even without market power, because scarce stored energy and uncertain future prices increase the value of holding energy. Second, the effect of uncertainty depends on the state of charge: when stored energy is scarce, greater uncertainty raises sell bid prices, whereas when stored energy is abundant it can lower them. Third, risk management reshapes bid curves into layered structures that secure profitable execution across a broad set of scenarios while preserving some exposure to rare but valuable price spikes.

</details>


### [104] [A New Primal-Dual Algorithm with Convex Combination and Extrapolation for Convex-Concave Saddle Point Problems with Nonlinear Coupling Term](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14220)
*Jialong Li,Zexian Liu,Xiaokai Chang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 针对带非线性耦合项的凸-凹鞍点问题中原始-对偶算法存在的几何不匹配、梯度信息延迟及强依赖线搜索等问题，本文提出结合凸组合与外推策略的新型原始-对偶算法PDAce及其加速版本aPDAce，在无需线搜索的条件下实现全局收敛，分别达到O(1/N)、O(1/N²)及线性收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有原始-对偶算法在处理带非线性耦合项的凸-凹鞍点问题时，存在变量空间与映射空间的几何不匹配、梯度信息延迟以及强依赖线搜索等问题，导致性能不稳定且收敛分析复杂。

Method: 提出PDAce算法：1) 在原始变量更新中构造凸组合点替代当前迭代点，并在该点计算非线性耦合项中向量函数的雅可比矩阵；2) 在对偶变量更新中利用最新的凸组合点信息，将外推从变量空间扩展到映射空间。核心创新在于在凸组合点处对非线性耦合项中的向量函数进行线性化，并将外推从变量空间转移到非线性映射空间。

Result: 在温和凸假设下，通过构造新的Lyapunov势函数证明PDAce全局收敛且遍历收敛率为O(1/N)；加速版本aPDAce在原始函数强凸时达到O(1/N²)率，在原始和对偶函数均强凸时达到线性收敛。

Conclusion: 所提方法彻底消除了非线性残差项，无需线搜索即可进行严格的收敛分析，性能更稳定。

Abstract: Convex-concave saddle point problems with nonlinear coupling term have wide applications in signal processing, machine learning, robust optimization, and generative models, among others. Primal-dual algorithms are widely used for convex-concave saddle point problems. However, when handling nonlinear coupling term in convex-concave saddle point problems, primal-dual algorithms usually encounter geometric mismatches between variable and mapping spaces, delayed gradient information, and strong dependence on linesearch, resulting in less stable performance and complex convergence analysis. To address these issues, we propose a new primal-dual algorithm named PDAce by combining convex combination and extrapolation strategies. More specifically, in the update of the primal variable, we construct a convex combination point to replace the current iterative point and compute the Jacobian matrix of the vector function in the nonlinear coupling term at the convex combination point. Besides, we use the latest information of convex combination points to extend extrapolation to the mapping space in the update of the dual variable. The core innovations lie in performing linearization of the vector function in the nonlinear coupling term at convex combination points and shifting extrapolation from the variable space to the nonlinear mapping space. This design completely eliminates nonlinear residual terms and allows for rigorous convergence analysis without linesearch. Under mild convex assumptions, we construct a new Lyapunov potential function to prove that PDAce is globally convergent with an ergodic convergence rate of $\mathcal{O}(1/N)$. Moreover, we develop an accelerated version of PDAce, termed aPDAce, which achieves $\mathcal{O}(1/N^2)$ rate under strong convexity of the primal function, and linear convergence when both the primal and dual functions are strongly convex.

</details>


### [105] [Operator Calculus for Population-Based Optimization: A Mean-Field Convergence Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14289)
*Pekka Malo,Lauri Viitasaari,Patrik Nummi,Antti Suominen,Ankur Sinha,Olli Tahvonen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种算子微积分框架，将一大类基于种群和分布的优化方法（如进化策略、共识优化、协方差矩阵适应等）统一描述为三个基本算子（变异、选择、重组）的复合作用，并建立了连续时间极限下的输运-反应-跳跃（TRJ）偏微分方程。基于此，作者提出了模块化Lyapunov原理：如果状态空间Lyapunov函数在全生成子下耗散且控制相关搜索空间度量，则状态空间Lyapunov泛函和搜索误差呈指数衰减。该框架为复合平均场算法的收敛性认证提供了模块化工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于种群和分布的优化方法（如进化策略、共识优化、协方差矩阵适应等）广泛应用于非凸或黑箱问题，但它们的收敛性分析依赖于碎片化的算法特定技术，缺乏统一的理论框架。

Method: 引入算子微积分，将优化方法描述为三个基本算子（变异、选择、重组）在概率测度上的复合作用；在稳定性和正则性条件下，复合算子的预生成子在连续时间极限下导出输运-反应-跳跃（TRJ）PDE；基于该框架建立模块化Lyapunov原理，通过算子级耗散估计组合来证明收敛性。

Result: 建立了模块化Lyapunov原理：若状态空间Lyapunov函数在全生成子下耗散且控制搜索空间度量，则Lyapunov泛函和搜索误差呈指数衰减。复合算子的加性生成子结构允许逐算子进行耗散估计。

Conclusion: 该算子微积分框架为多种分布优化方法提供了统一的收敛性分析工具，通过模块化方式认证复合平均场算法的指数收敛性。

Abstract: Population-based and distributional optimization methods, from evolution strategies and consensus-based optimization to covariance-matrix adaptation and stochastic gradient methods viewed as distributional dynamics, are widely used for nonconvex or black-box problems, yet their convergence analyses remain fragmented across algorithm-specific techniques. We introduce an operator calculus in which a broad class of such methods, after choosing an appropriate state space and, where necessary, augmenting the state by memory or strategy variables, is described as a composition of three elementary operators (mutation, selection, and recombination) acting on probability measures. Under explicit stability and regularity conditions, the composite operator admits a pre-generator whose continuous-time limit is a transport-reaction-jump (TRJ) PDE that preserves the operator splitting. On this foundation we establish a modular Lyapunov principle. If a state-space Lyapunov function both dissipates under the full generator and controls the relevant search-space gauges, then the state-space Lyapunov functional and the induced search errors decay exponentially. The additive generator structure allows dissipation estimates to be assembled operator by operator, providing a toolkit for certifying convergence of composite mean-field algorithms.

</details>


### [106] [A simulation study on spatial exponential decay of perturbations in a two-dimensional wave equation with optimal boundary/line control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14410)
*Benedikt Oppeneiger*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文通过二维波动方程的最优边界控制仿真，研究控制几何对扰动空间指数衰减的影响。结果表明：仅在内部线控制网格（结合边界控制）场景下观察到域一致的空间指数衰减，而纯边界控制无法实现，这是由于有限传播速度要求控制域间距有上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受一维双曲PDE中域一致可镇定/可检测性蕴含扰动空间指数衰减的结论启发，探究该效应在更高维度的最优边界控制中是否成立。

Method: 对二维波动方程在扩张方形域上进行仿真，分别比较纯外边界控制和内线网格+边界控制两种几何；空间用P1有限元离散，时间用隐式中点法，采用缩聚KKT系统+预处理共轭梯度法求解。

Result: 仅在内线网格控制场景下观察到域一致的扰动空间指数衰减，纯边界控制无法实现该衰减特性。

Conclusion: 有限传播速度要求控制域间距存在一致上界，因此需要分布式内部控制才能实现域一致的空间指数衰减。

Abstract: Recent results have shown that domain-uniform stabilizability and detectability imply spatial exponential decay of perturbations in optimally controlled hyperbolic PDEs on one-dimensional domains. This domain-uniform stabilizability and detectability can be achieved only if the control domain is distributed over the whole spatial domain such that the distance between neighboring control intervals is bounded from above. Motivated by these insights we investigate whether analogous effects can be observed for optimal boundary control in higher dimensions. For this purpose we conduct a simulation study on a two-dimensional wave equation on expanding square domains which is driven by a localized perturbation of the initial displacement. We compare two control geometries: a control acting only on the outer boundary and a control acting on a regular grid of interior line interfaces combined with the boundary. The problem is discretized by conforming P1 finite elements in space and the implicit midpoint rule in time. Instead of assembling the full space-time KKT system, we use a condensed formulation and solve the reduced optimality system by preconditioned conjugate gradients. We show that domain-uniform spatial decay of the perturbation can only be observed in the scenario with a regular grid of line controls. This is because finite propagation velocity requires a uniform bound on the distance that a wave can travel without reaching the control domain.

</details>


### [107] [Free Heavy-Tailed Lunch for Muon: A Theoretical Justification of Empirical Success](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14560)
*Florian Hübler,Thomas Pethick,Suvrit Sra*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文在重尾非凸随机优化框架下，理论证明了Muon等非欧优化方法相比欧几里得方法在样本复杂度上的维度优势，并实验验证了其在大语言模型训练中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非欧优化方法（如Muon、Scion）在训练Transformer模型时表现优异，但其相较于欧几里得方法的理论优势尚不明确，作者希望在重尾非凸条件下填补这一理论空白。

Method: 在随机梯度具有有界p阶中心矩（p∈(1,2]）的重尾非凸设定下，分析非欧方法在核范数驻点条件下的样本复杂度，并与欧几里得方法进行对比，同时给出最优性下界证明。

Result: Muon达到ε-驻点的样本复杂度为O(min{m,n}·Δ₁L/ε²·(σ/ε)^{p/(p-1)})，该复杂度对所有一阶方法在核范数驻点度量下是最优的；实验中在大语言模型上验证了理论。

Conclusion: 非欧方法在重尾非凸优化中具有维度无关的样本效率优势，且除Muon的谱几何外，其他Schatten几何也可能在某些场景下具有竞争力。

Abstract: Non-Euclidean optimisation methods with matrix-valued updates, such as Muon and Scion, have recently shown strong empirical performance for training Transformer models, yet their theoretical advantages over Euclidean methods remain poorly understood. We address this gap in the heavy-tailed non-convex regime, where stochastic gradients have bounded $p$-th central moments, $p \in (1,2]$. We show that certain non-Euclidean methods achieve optimal sample complexity under stronger stationarity measures, while Euclidean methods incur additional dimension-dependent costs. As a consequence, for $m \times n$ matrices, Muon finds an $\varepsilon$-stationary point in nuclear norm within $\mathcal{O}\left(\min\{m, n\} \frac{Δ_1 L}{\varepsilon^2} \left(\frac σ\varepsilon \right)^{\frac p {p-1}}\right)$ samples, absorbing heavy-tailed noise without extra dimension dependence, unlike Euclidean methods. We further prove this sample complexity, including its dimension dependence, is optimal for all first-order methods under nuclear-norm stationarity. Experiments on large language models support our theory. Surprisingly, our results suggest that other Schatten geometries beyond the spectral geometry of Muon can perform competitively in certain settings.

</details>

### [108] [Altermagnetism and bond-nematicity in the spin-$1/2$ square lattice $J_1-J_2-δ$ model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14101)
*Tanja Đurić,Shenlong Yu,Pinaki Sengupta*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用结合对称增强神经网络和变分蒙特卡洛的机器学习方法，研究了自旋-1/2方形晶格$J_1-J_2-\delta$模型中，由几何阻挫增强和量子涨落驱动的交变磁序熔化所导致的键向列相出现。在交换调制较大且阻挫增强时，发现了共存的对称保护拓扑价键固体和键向列序，其特征为磁子对凝聚、U(1)自旋旋转及$\mathbb{Z}_2$自旋反转对称性破缺，以及激发谱中三子能级的宇称劈裂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对交变磁材料的研究主要集中在交变磁矩对电子能带结构和超导等性质的影响，而强量子涨落效应以及交变磁序熔化可能导致的物相尚缺乏探索。本文旨在填补这一空白，研究交变磁序熔化后可能出现的新型奇异物相。

Method: 采用结合对称增强神经网络架构与变分蒙特卡洛的新型机器学习方法，研究自旋-1/2方形晶格$J_1-J_2-\delta$模型。该模型在弱几何阻挫区具有交变磁序，在强阻挫和小交换调制参数$\delta$区存在无能隙自旋液体相。

Result: 在交换调制较大、导致交变磁区中不同手性磁子模显著劈裂的情况下，增加阻挫使交变磁序熔化后出现一个有趣的物相——共存的对称保护拓扑价键固体和键向列序。该相的特征包括：磁子对凝聚导致键向列性、U(1)自旋旋转和$\mathbb{Z}_2$自旋反转对称性破缺，以及激发谱中三子能级的手性劈裂。

Conclusion: 本研究识别出交变磁序附近可能出现的新型奇异物相（共存拓扑价键固体和键向列序），为理解交变磁材料中强量子涨落诱导的物相行为迈出了重要一步。

Abstract: We study appearance of bond-nematicity in insulating altermagnetic materials induced by increased frustration and quantum-fluctuations driven melting of the altermagnetic order. Using novel machine learning approach that combines symmetry enhanced neural network architectures and variational Monte Carlo we consider the spin-$1/2$ square lattice $J_1-J_2-δ$ model known to have altermagnetic ordering in the regime of small geometric frustration and a gapless spin liquid phase in the regime of strong frustration and small exchange interaction modulation parameter $δ$. In the regime where exchange modulation is relatively large, resulting in the significant splitting of the magnon modes with different chiralities in the altermagnetic regime, we find that melting of the altermagnetic order by increased frustration leads to an intriguing phase that hosts coexisting symmetry protected topological valence bond solid and bond-nematic orders. The phase is characterized by condensation of magnon pairs that results in bond-nematicity, breaking of U(1) spin rotation and $\mathbb{Z}_2$ spin inversion symmetries and chiral splitting of the triplon-like energy levels in the excitation spectrum. Whilst numerous recent studies address non-trivial impact of altermagnetic moments on various properties in altermagnetic materials, like electronic band structure and superconductivity, influence of strong quantum fluctuations and phases that can result from melting of the altermagnetic order are much less explored. Our study therefore presents an important step in identifying exotic phases of matter that can emerge in vicinity of the altermagnetic order.

</details>


### [109] [Ferromagnetic Order of Reduced Magnetic Moments in a Frustrated Sawtooth Chain of the Magnetic Semiconductor ZnYb$_2$S$_4$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14196)
*Shinji Okada,Hiroto Suzuki,Nonoka Higa,Yasuyuki Shimura,Takanori Taniguchi,Takahiro Onimaru*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了正交半导体ZnYb₂S₄中Yb³⁺离子形成的锯齿链（sawtooth chain）的磁性。比热在1.4 K出现尖峰，但磁熵仅释放了Rln2的27%；磁化曲线显示0.1 μB/Yb的自发磁化，远小于预期；中子衍射未发现反铁磁超晶格反射。结果表明基态为铁磁排列，但磁受挫显著抑制了磁矩幅度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索锯齿自旋链中竞争最近邻和次近邻相互作用导致的长程有序抑制及新奇量子态（如自旋二聚体单态、1/2磁化平台、自旋收缩）的实验实现，通过研究ZnYb₂S₄这一具体材料体系来揭示磁受挫效应。

Method: 对ZnYb₂S₄单晶/多晶样品进行比热测量、等温磁化测量（0.28 K）以及粉末中子衍射实验，分析其热力学和磁学性质。

Result: 比热在Tm=1.4 K出现尖峰，磁熵仅达Rln2的27%；等温磁化显示0.2 T以下磁滞回线，自发磁化仅0.1 μB/Yb；中子衍射在T<Tm时未观察到反铁磁超晶格反射。

Conclusion: ZnYb₂S₄中Yb³⁺锯齿链在基态呈铁磁排列，但由于锯齿几何带来的磁受挫效应，磁矩幅度被显著抑制（仅为预期的十分之一），基态为受挫铁磁态而非反铁磁有序。

Abstract: In a sawtooth spin chain, competing nearest- and next-nearest-neighbor interactions suppress long-range order, yielding novel quantum states such as a spin-dimer singlet, 1/2 magnetization plateau, and spin contraction. Here, we investigate the magnetic properties of the orthorhombic semiconductor ZnYb$_2$S$_4$, in which Yb$^{3+}$ ions with an effective spin-1/2 form a sawtooth chain along the $b$-axis. The specific heat exhibits a sharp peak at ${T}_{\rm m}$ $=$ 1.4 K, at which the magnetic entropy $S_{\rm m}$ reaches only 27% of $R$ln2. This reduced $S_{\rm m}$ at $T_{\rm m}$ indicates the entropy release of the ground state doublet of Yb$^{3+}$ even for $T$ $>$ $T_{\rm m}$. The isothermal magnetization $M(B)$ at 0.28 K exhibits hysteresis for $\left|B\right| \leq 0.2$ T and increases monotonically for $B > 0.2$ T. The spontaneous magnetization is only 0.1 ${\it μ}_{\rm B}$$/$Yb, an order of magnitude smaller than that expected for the ground state doublet of Yb$^{3+}$. Moreover, in powder neutron diffraction measurements, no superlattice reflections due to antiferromagnetic order are observed for $T$ $<$ $T_{\rm m}$. Therefore, in the ground state, the Yb moments are ferromagnetically aligned, but their amplitude is reduced by magnetic frustration in the sawtooth Yb chain.

</details>


### [110] [Bond-operator analytical approach for the $t$-$J$ model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14374)
*A. V. Syromyatnikov*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种键算符理论（BOT），用于分析t-J模型及其含长程跳跃项的扩展。该技术基于电子算符通过局域自旋1/2和无约束的移动空穴费米子算符的表示，通过引入在(扩展)元胞中作用的玻色子和费米子算符，提供了物理量的1/n正则展开。在n=1时，即使一阶近似也能与数值结果良好吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有处理t-J模型的解析方法存在局限性，如约束条件难以处理、无法统一描述磁有序和无序相、对两空穴束缚态（库珀对）等复合激发描述复杂。本文旨在发展一种系统的、可正则展开的解析理论方法。

Method: 基于电子算符通过局域自旋1/2和无约束的自旋费米子算符的表示，在包含多个格点的(扩展)元胞中引入玻色子和费米子算符，构建键算符理论。利用1/n展开的常规图技术进行正则微扰计算，其中n为元胞中可占据的最大准粒子数。

Result: 在n=1时，即使仅考虑一阶1/n展开和少数简单图，也能与之前的数值结果良好定量吻合。自洽计算进一步使基态能量、交错磁化强度、磁子谱、极化子谱和最低能两空穴束缚态谱与数值结果达到非常好的定量一致。

Conclusion: BOT提供了一种统一的解析框架描述t-J模型的磁有序和无序相，其中某些复合激发（如两空穴束缚态）在BOT中由独立玻色子描述，避免了传统方法中将其视为束缚态的复杂性。尽管物理重要情形n=1时1/n展开参数并不小，该方法仍能取得良好定量结果。

Abstract: We present a bond-operator theory (BOT) for analytical consideration of the $t$-$J$ model and its extensions with longer-rage hopping terms. This technique is based on previously suggested representation of electron operators via localized spins 1/2 and spinless Fermi-operators of mobile holons which requires no constraint between them. We introduce a representation of operators of spins, holons, and electrons in (extended) unit cell containing several lattice sites via a zoo of Bose- and Fermi-operators acting in the Hilbert space of all quantum states of the whole unit cell. BOT provides a regular expansion of physical quantities in powers of $1/n$ using conventional diagrammatic technique, where $n\ge1$ is the maximum number of introduced quasiparticles (bosons and fermions) which can occupy a unit cell. The suggested representation reproduces commutation algebra of all operators at any $n>0$ and allows to consider both magnetically ordered and disordered phases. Some elementary excitations described in the BOT by separate bosons or fermions appear in common approaches as bound states of conventional quasiparticles. In particular, there are two-hole bound states (Cooper pairs of two holes) which are described within the BOT by separate bosons. We discuss in detail properties of the $t$-$J$ model on the square lattice with no more than two holes (polarons). Although the expansion parameter $1/n$ is not small in the physically meaningful case of $n=1$, we obtain a good quantitative agreement with previous numerical findings at $n=1$ even in the first order in $1/n$ after taking into account a few simple diagrams. Self-consistent calculations in the first order in $1/n$ bring our results to a very good quantitative agreement with previous numerical findings of the ground state energy, staggered magnetization, and spectra of magnons, polarons, and lowest-energy two-hole bound states.

</details>


### [111] [SmoQyElPhQMC.jl: An open-source Julia package for efficient and scalable quantum Monte Carlo simulations of electron-phonon coupled models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14425)
*Benjamin Cohen-Stead,James Neuhaus,Kipton Barros,Thomas A. Maier,Steven Johnston*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文介绍了SmoQyElPhQMC v1.0，一个基于Julia的开源包，用于对电子-声子耦合模型哈密顿量进行可扩展的量子蒙特卡洛模拟，实现了线性标度算法，并提供了灵活的脚本接口和与Julia生态系统的集成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提供一种可扩展、高效的开源工具，用于模拟电子-声子耦合模型，解决此类模拟在系统尺寸和逆温度上的计算瓶颈问题。

Method: 基于SmoQyDQMC代码库，实现了改进版本的算法，支持在系统尺寸和逆温度上线性标度的模拟，并扩展了灵活的脚本接口。

Result: 发布了SmoQyElPhQMC v1.0，支持对广泛的非关联电子-声子模型进行线性标度模拟，代码开源托管于GitHub，并提供完整的在线文档和示例。

Conclusion: SmoQyElPhQMC为电子-声子耦合系统的量子蒙特卡洛模拟提供了一个高效、可扩展且用户友好的开源解决方案，便于集成到不同的工作流程中。

Abstract: We introduce version 1.0 of the SmoQyElPhQMC package, an open-source Julia code for performing scalable quantum Monte Carlo simulations of electron-phonon coupled model Hamiltonians. SmoQyElPhQMC is built upon the SmoQyDQMC codebase and implements improved versions of the algorithms presented in [B. Cohen-Stead \textit{et al}., Phys. Rev. E {\bf 105}, 065302 (2022)] to enable linear-scaling simulations of a broad class of uncorrelated $e$-ph models both in system size and inverse temperature. By extending the functionality of the flexible scripting interface introduced in SmoQyDQMC, the SmoQyElPhQMC package continues to allow users to adapt it to different workflows and interface with other software packages in the Julia ecosystem. The code for this package can be downloaded from our GitHub repository at https://github.com/SmoQySuite/SmoQyElPhQMC.jl or installed using the Julia package manager. The online documentation, including examples, can be obtained from our documentation page at https://smoqysuite.github.io/SmoQyElPhQMC.jl/stable/.

</details>


### [112] [Open Wilson chain numerical renormalization group approach to steady-state non-equilibrium quantum transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14635)
*Anand Manaparambil,Frithjof B. Anders*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将数值重整化群(NRG)扩展到非平衡态的方法——开放链全密度矩阵(OC-FDM)方法。该方法通过为每个Wilson位点增加一个储库，并利用连分式展开精确恢复连续极限，通过Bloch-Redfield张量的零本征向量获得稳态密度算符。该方法在量子点电荷和自旋输运研究中，能探索低至$T_K/D \approx 10^{-8}$的Kondo能标，与平衡态基准完美吻合，且单数据点计算时间比现有方法少数个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平衡态下NRG已能高精度识别量子杂质系统的平衡态，但非平衡态下稳态密度算符不再服从玻尔兹曼形式，而是由边界条件决定。需要将NRG扩展到非平衡态以处理有限偏压和温度梯度下的输运问题。

Method: 在每个Wilson位点上附加一个储库，通过连分式展开计算耦合函数以精确恢复连续极限；非平衡参数（有限偏压、温度梯度）通过Bloch-Redfield张量引入，其零本征向量给出稳态密度算符；采用有效单引线描述实现OC-FDM方法。

Result: OC-FDM方法能探索低至$T_K/D \approx 10^{-8}$的Kondo能标；与平衡态FDM-NRG和费米液体标度定量吻合；与实时间量子蒙特卡洛(RT-QMC)在电荷涨落等局域性质上吻合极好；单数据点在标准笔记本上计算时间比现有方法少数个数量级。

Conclusion: OC-FDM方法成功将NRG扩展到非平衡态，能高效、高精度地研究量子杂质系统在有限偏压和温度梯度下的输运性质及局域性质，计算效率远超现有方法。

Abstract: The numerical renormalization group (NRG) approach was developed to identify and quantify different equilibrium regimes of quantum impurity systems (QISs) with unprecedented accuracy by a tailored finite size representation. Out of equilibrium, the steady-state density operator is not of the Boltzmannian form but one that is determined by the imposed boundary conditions. We extend the NRG to the nonequilibrium setting by augmenting each Wilson site with a reservoir, whose coupling functions are calculated via a continuous fraction expansion in order to recover the continuum limit exactly. The nonequilibrium parameters such as a finite bias as well as a finite temperature gradient enters through the Bloch-Redfield tensor (BRT), whose zero eigenvector gives the steady-state density operator. We used the resulting open chain full density matrix (OC-FDM) approach with an effective single lead description to investigate the charge and spin transport through a quantum dot (QD) under finite bias and temperature gradient. The influence of lead asymmetry and an external magnetic field on the transport properties are also studied. For completeness, we have also investigated local properties of the QD, such as charge fluctuations and find excellent agreement with real-time quantum Monte Carlo (RT-QMC) data. The OC-FDM approach was able to explore Kondo energy scales as low as $T_K/D \approx 10^{-8}$ in the non-equilibrium regime, as well as show convergence with the established equilibrium benchmarks, such as a quantitative agreement with full density matrix numerical renormalization group (FDM-NRG) and Fermi-liquid scaling at small bias and temperature. Owing to the effective single lead description, a single OC-FDM data point takes orders of magnitude less time on a standard laptop, compared to other state-of-the-art numerical methods.

</details>

# math.RT [[Back]](#toc)

### [113] [Simple modules over the superconformal algebra $\mathcal{S}^{\prime}(1,n)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14099)
*Jinxin Hu,Rencai Lü,Xinyue Wang*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究超共形代数 $\mathcal{S}^\prime(1,n)$ 上的 Shen-Larsson 模，给出了其单性的充要条件及非单情形下的子商结构，并利用该结果结合已有分类，显式构造了所有单尖模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Shen-Larsson模是连接Weyl超代数与特殊线性李超代数的重要构造，但其单性条件尚未明确，且其在单尖模分类中的应用有待挖掘。

Method: 利用Weyl超代数 $K_{1,n}$ 和 $\mathfrak{sl}(1,n)$ 上的模构造Shen-Larsson模，通过分析其结构建立单性的充要条件，并研究非单情形下的单子商；进而结合Martínez-Mathieu-Zelmanov的分类定理，给出所有单尖模的显式构造。

Result: 给出了Shen-Larsson模单性的充要条件，并在非单情形下刻画了其单子商；利用此结果，显式构造了 $\mathcal{S}^\prime(1,n)$ 上的所有单尖模。

Conclusion: Shen-Larsson模的结构完全由底层Weyl超代数模和 $\mathfrak{sl}(1,n)$ 模决定，且通过该框架可统一描述超共形代数 $\mathcal{S}^\prime(1,n)$ 的全部单尖模。

Abstract: Let $n\geq 2$, and let $\mathcal{S}(1,n)$ be the Lie superalgebra of zero-superdivergence superderivations of $\mathbb{C}[t^{\pm1}]\otimesΛ(n)$. Its derived algebra $\mathcal{S}^\prime(1,n):=[\mathcal{S}(1,n),\mathcal{S}(1,n)]$ is well known as a superconformal algebra. In this paper, we first study Shen-Larsson modules over $\mathcal{S}^\prime(1,n)$. These modules, introduced by G. Shen and T. A. Larsson, are constructed from modules over the Weyl superalgebra $K_{1,n}$ and the special linear Lie superalgebra $\mathfrak{sl}(1,n)$. We establish necessary and sufficient conditions for the simplicity of Shen-Larsson modules and investigate their simple subquotients in the non-simple case. Then as an application, building on the classification of simple cuspidal $\mathcal{S}^\prime(1,n)$-modules by C. Martínez, O. Mathieu and E. Zelmanov, we obtain an explicit construction of all simple cuspidal modules over $\mathcal{S}^\prime(1,n)$.

</details>


### [114] [Quantum dynamical Weyl groups from quantum loop groups of arbitrary shuffle type](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14262)
*Tianqing Zhu*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文构造了任意洗牌型量子环群的量子动力学Weyl群元，进而定义了量子格点算子和代数量子差分方程，证明了这些算子相互对易且与qKZ算子对易，可视为三角Casimir联络的差分类比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将量子动力学Weyl群元的构造推广到任意洗牌型量子环群，并建立其与量子格点模型和量子差分方程的联系。

Method: 通过构造量子动力学Weyl群元，定义量子格点算子和代数量子差分方程；证明对易性并与qKZ算子对易性。

Result: 代数量子差分算子构成一族相互对易的算子，且与qKZ算子对易。

Conclusion: 该工作恢复并推广了OS22的构造，可视为有限型对称Cartan矩阵对应的三角Casimir联络的差分类比。

Abstract: We construct quantum dynamical Weyl group elements associated with quantum loop groups of arbitrary shuffle type. Using the construction, we define the quantum lattice operator and the algebraic quantum difference equations for each tensor products of semisimple modules $V$ in category $\mathcal{O}$. We prove that algebraic quantum difference operators form a family of commuting operators, and they also commute with the qKZ operators for the tensor products of modules of the above type in $\mathcal{O}$. This recovers the construction in \cite{OS22} and can be viewed as the difference analog of the trigonmetric Casimir connection when the quantum loop group corresponds to the finite type symmetrisable Cartan matrix.

</details>


### [115] [A proof of Harish-Chandra's integrability theorem for cuspidal representations of $\mathrm{GL}_n(\mathbb F_\ell((t)))$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14279)
*Avraham Aizenbud,Nir Avni,Dmitry Gourevitch,David Kazhdan,Eitan Sayag*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文证明了对 $F=\mathbb{F}_\ell((t))$ 上的 Chevalley 映射 $p: \mathfrak{gl}_n(F) \to (\mathfrak{gl}_n//\mathrm{GL}_n)(F)$，光滑紧支撑测度在 $p$ 下的推前测度的密度属于所有 $L^q$ 空间；进而结合 [AGKSc] 的主要结果，得到 $\mathrm{GL}_n(F)$ 上不可约尖点表示的 Harish-Chandra 特征标的局部可积性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立 $\mathfrak{gl}_n(F)$ 上光滑紧支撑测度经 Chevalley 映射推前后的 $L^q$ 可积性，以推导 $\mathrm{GL}_n(F)$ 上不可约尖点表示的 Harish-Chandra 特征标的局部可积性。

Method: 利用 Chevalley 映射 $p$ 将 $\mathfrak{gl}_n(F)$ 上的光滑紧支撑测度推前到商空间，并分析推前测度的密度函数在 $L^q$ 空间中的性质。

Result: 推前测度的密度函数对任意有限 $q$ 均属于 $L^q$ 空间；由此推出 Harish-Chandra 特征标满足局部可积性。

Conclusion: 该结果推进了对局部域上约化群表示理论中特征标分析的理解，为尖点表示的特征标局部可积性提供了新的证明路径。

Abstract: Consider the Chevalley map $$ p:\mathfrak{gl} _n(F)\to (\mathfrak{gl}_n//\mathrm{GL}_n)(F), $$ where $F=\mathbb{F}_\ell((t))$. We show that the push forward via $p$ of every smooth compactly supported measure on $\mathfrak{gl}_n(F)$ is a measure whose density belongs to $L^q$ for every finite $q$.
  As a consequence, using the main result of [AGKSc], we obtain local integrability for Harish--Chandra's characters of irreducible cuspidal representations of $\mathrm{GL}_n(F)$.

</details>


### [116] [A nonabelian Fourier transform for tempered unipotent representations, II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14485)
*Anne-Marie Aubert,Dan Ciubotaru,Beth Romano*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文将非阿贝尔傅里叶变换从单位幂椭圆特征空间扩展到约化p进群的单位幂紧致特征空间，研究了其与极大紧开子群分支的关系，并证明了该变换在SL_n、GL_n和G_2等群上与抛物限制的兼容性，同时通过抛物诱导建立了紧致设定与椭圆设定之间的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将前期工作中提出的非阿贝尔傅里叶变换从单位幂椭圆特征空间推广到更一般的单位幂紧致特征空间，并探索其与极大紧开子群分支及抛物限制的关系。

Method: 通过扩展非阿贝尔傅里叶变换的定义域，研究其与极大紧开子群的限制表示的关系，利用抛物诱导建立紧致设定与椭圆设定之间的联系，并在SL_n、GL_n和G_2等群上验证该变换与抛物限制的兼容性。

Result: 成功将非阿贝尔傅里叶变换扩展到单位幂紧致特征空间，证明了该变换在SL_n、GL_n和G_2等群上与抛物限制兼容，并通过抛物诱导建立了紧致设定与椭圆设定之间的关系。

Conclusion: 非阿贝尔傅里叶变换可以自然地推广到紧致设定，并且与抛物限制和抛物诱导等重要操作具有兼容性，为约化p进群表示论中的傅里叶分析提供了更广泛的理论框架。

Abstract: We extend the construction of the nonabelian Fourier transform proposed in our previous paper from the space of unipotent elliptic characters to the space of unipotent compact characters of a reductive $p$-adic group, and we investigate its relation with the branching to maximal compact open subgroups. We prove that this Fourier transform is compatible with parahoric restriction for certain groups, including $\mathrm{SL}_n, \mathrm{GL}_n$, and $G_2$. We also relate the compact setting to the elliptic setting using parabolic induction.

</details>


### [117] [Theory of Infinite Maximal Green Sequences: Finest Stability Data, CBHO Sequence and Cluster quiver pattern](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14552)
*Fang Li,Kangping Qu*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文在阿贝尔长度范畴中引入无限长极大绿色序列，建立了无限极大绿色序列、最优稳定性数据和完备反向Hom-正交砖序列之间的自然双射。对于无限秩的簇箭图模式（等价于簇代数），通过c-向量定义了无限长极大绿色序列，并证明在适当条件下可通过特定构造将簇箭图模式中的极大绿色序列范畴化为对应表示范畴中的挠类极大绿色序列，从而建立了表示论中无限极大绿色序列与无限秩簇代数的极大绿色序列之间的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将有限长极大绿色序列的概念推广至无限长情形，并建立无限秩簇代数中极大绿色序列与表示论中相应概念的对应关系。

Method: 在阿贝尔长度范畴中形式化定义无限长极大绿色序列，证明其与最优稳定性数据及CBHO砖序列的双射关系；对无限秩簇箭图模式，利用c-向量定义极大绿色序列，并通过特定构造实现范畴化。

Result: 建立了无限极大绿色序列、最优稳定性数据、CBHO砖序列三者间的自然双射；在适当条件下实现了无限秩簇箭图模式中极大绿色序列到表示范畴中挠类极大绿色序列的范畴化。

Conclusion: 该框架连接了表示论中的无限极大绿色序列与无限秩簇代数的极大绿色序列，为两者之间建立了桥梁。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce maximal green sequences of infinite length in abelian length categories and establish natural bijections among infinite maximal green sequences, finest stability data, and complete backward $\operatorname{Hom}$-orthogonal (briefly, CBHO) sequences of bricks.
  For cluster quiver patterns (equivalently, cluster algebras) of infinite rank, we define maximal green sequences of infinite length via $c$-vectors. Moreover, we prove that under suitable conditions such a maximal green sequence in a cluster quiver pattern of infinite rank can be categorified as a maximal green sequence of torsion classes in the corresponding representation category via a specific construction, thereby providing an example of a maximal green sequence in an abelian length category. This framework establishes a connection between infinite maximal green sequences in representation theory and maximal green sequences for cluster algebras of infinite rank.

</details>


### [118] [Quasi-Classical Braverman--Kazhdan Intertwiners via Quiver Varieties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14637)
*Nikolay Grantcharov,Aaron Slipper*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文构造了标准抛物子群P和P'之间的一种同构关系，该同构作用于Braverman-Kazhdan空间的余切丛的仿射化，并证明其满足SL_n×L^ab-等变性和辫关系。这些同构是Braverman-Kazhdan正规化交织算子的准经典极限，并通过定义SL-规范群的箭图范畴的反射函子实现，推广了Wang在P=B情形下通过箭图范畴构造Gelfand-Graev作用的工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立标准抛物子群P和P'（其Levi子群共轭）之间的几何同构，将Braverman-Kazhdan正规化交织算子推广到准经典层面，并推广Wang关于Gelfand-Graev作用的构造。

Method: 通过定义带有SL-规范群的箭图范畴的反射函子，构造仿射化余切丛之间的同构Φ(P,P')，并验证SL_n×L^ab-等变性和辫关系。

Result: 构造了同构Φ(P,P'): T*(SL_n/[P,P])的仿射化 → T*(SL_n/[P',P'])的仿射化，满足等变性和辫关系，且是Braverman-Kazhdan正规化交织算子的准经典极限。

Conclusion: 成功建立了标准抛物子群之间的几何同构，统一并推广了相关构造，为量子群表示论和几何表示论提供了新工具。

Abstract: Let $P$ and $P'$ be standard parabolic subgroups of $\mathrm{SL}_n(\mathbb{C})$ whose Levi subgroups are conjugate. We construct isomorphisms $Φ(P,P'):\overline{T^*(\mathrm{SL}_n/[P,P])}^{\mathrm{aff}}\rightarrow\overline{T^*(\mathrm{SL}_n/[P',P'])}^{\mathrm{aff}}$ between the affinizations of the cotangent bundles of the Braverman--Kazhdan spaces $\mathrm{SL}_n/[P,P]$ and $\mathrm{SL}_n/[P',P']$, and we show that they satisfy $\mathrm{SL}_n\times L^{\mathrm{ab}}$-equivariance and braid relations. These isomorphisms are the quasi-classical limits of Braverman--Kazhdan normalized intertwining operators. Our construction proceeds by defining reflection functors for quiver varieties with an $\mathrm{SL}$-gauge group, thereby generalizing Wang's construction of the Gelfand--Graev action on $\overline{T^*(\mathrm{SL}_n/U)}^{\mathrm{aff}}$ via quiver varieties in the case $P=B$.

</details>

### [119] [Optimal binning of $D\rightarrow K_{\mathrm S}^0π^+π^-$ and $D\rightarrow K_{\mathrm S}^0K^+K^-$ phase space for experimental measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13948)
*Marcelo Bovill,Nathan Jurik,Sneha Malde*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出D→K⁰_Sπ⁺π⁻和D→K⁰_SK⁺K⁻衰变Dalitz图的新分箱方案，通过优化品质因子提升γ测量精度约5%，并首次针对D→K⁰_Sπ⁺π⁻中的粲混合观测量x_CP和Δx进行专用分箱优化，统计灵敏度提升约20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分箱方案在γ测量灵敏度上存在不足，且未充分考虑与其他物理参数的简并效应和本底处理；同时缺乏针对粲混合观测量x_CP和Δx的专用优化方案。

Method: 提出新的品质因子以更好地表征γ测量灵敏度，纳入参数简并效应和更精确的本底处理；针对粲混合观测量，同时考虑统计灵敏度和探测器分辨导致的bin迁移效应进行优化。

Result: γ测量精度预期提升约5%；粲混合观测量统计灵敏度提升约20%，bin迁移偏差保持在与当前方案相当的水平。

Conclusion: 新分箱方案在γ测量和粲混合观测量的统计灵敏度方面均有显著改进，为B±→DK±及D介子混合相关测量提供更优的实验方案。

Abstract: New binning schemes for the Dalitz plots of $D \rightarrow K^0_{\mathrm S}π^+π^-$ and $D \rightarrow K^0_{\mathrm S} K^+ K^-$ decays are presented. These are determined using a new figure of merit for optimisation that better represents the sensitivity to $γ$ than previous metrics. Further augmentation comes from including consideration of degeneracy with other physics parameters determined simultaneously and a more accurate treatment of relevant backgrounds. The overall expected improvement in the precision of $γ$ measurements using $B^\pm\to DK^\pm$ decays, followed by $D \rightarrow K^0_{\mathrm S}π^+π^-$, is estimated at around 5$\%$. In addition, the first dedicated optimisation is performed for the observables relevant to charm mixing, $x_{CP}$ and $Δx$, in $D \rightarrow K^0_{\mathrm S}π^+π^-$ decays. This procedure accounts for both statistical sensitivity and the migration of events between bins due to detector resolution. The resulting binning scheme leads to an estimated gain of approximately 20$\%$ in statistical sensitivity, while maintaining the bias due to bin migration at a level comparable to the currently used scheme.

</details>

Ziembicki,E. D. Zimmerman,M. Zito,S. Zsoldos*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文详细介绍了T2K实验中ND280离线近探测器在约束中微子通量和中微子-原子核相互作用截面方面的方法学与结果，展示了新模型参数化、数据拟合、鲁棒性验证以及未来升级探测器对系统误差抑制的灵敏度研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在T2K实验的中微子振荡测量中，需要精确约束入射中微子通量及中微子-原子核相互作用截面，以控制振荡参数拟合中的系统不确定性，确保测量结果的可靠性。

Method: 采用新的中微子相互作用截面模型和精细化的通量预测进行参数化，将其拟合至ND280新事件选择数据；同时进行了大量鲁棒性研究，包括使用替代相互作用模型拟合、与公开截面测量数据的一致性检验以及p值评估。

Result: 实现了对振荡参数拟合中系统不确定性的稳健控制；验证了方法学的可靠性与鲁棒性；灵敏度研究显示，经过升级的ND280探测器（具有改进的接收度和更低强子阈值）可进一步增强约束能力，进一步降低系统误差。

Conclusion: 基于ND280的约束方法学有效且稳健，未来探测器升级有望进一步提升系统误差抑制效果，从而改善中微子振荡参数的测量精度。

Abstract: In the context of neutrino oscillation measurements from the T2K experiment, the off-axis near detector ND280 plays a crucial role in constraining the incoming neutrino flux and neutrino-nucleus interaction cross sections. The result is a robust control over systematic uncertainties in the fit of neutrino oscillation parameters to the data at the T2K far detector, Super-Kamiokande. This paper details the methodology and results of these constraints in the context of the latest neutrino oscillation analysis from T2K. It describes how a new neutrino cross-section model and refined flux prediction are parameterized and fit to data in new ND280 event selections. Additionally, this work reports the results of extensive robustness studies, including fits with alternative interaction models, consistency checks against publicly available cross-section measurements, and \textit{p}-value evaluations, to demonstrate the reliability and robustness of our methodology. Finally, we present a sensitivity study demonstrating that the upgraded ND280, with improved acceptance and a lower hadron threshold, may enhance future constraints and further reduce systematic uncertainties in oscillation measurements.

</details>

Zhu,Lin Zhu,S. H. Zhu,T. J. Zhu,W. D. Zhu,W. J. Zhu,W. Z. Zhu,Y. C. Zhu,Z. A. Zhu,X. Y. Zhuang,M. Zhuge,J. H. Zou,J. Zu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: BESIII合作组首次观测到ψ(3770)强子跃迁到π⁰J/ψ的过程，统计显著性为6.1σ（其中ψ(3770)→π⁰J/ψ的显著性为4.5σ），测得分支比为(2.08 ± 0.36 ± 0.21 ± 0.25)×10⁻⁵，该结果显著低于四夸克模型预言，但与介子圈效应计算一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 首次实验确定ψ(3770)→π⁰J/ψ的强子跃迁分支比，检验四夸克模型与介子圈效应对ψ(3770)同位旋破缺衰变的预言。

Method: 利用BESIII探测器在√s=3.773 GeV处收集的20.3 fb⁻¹数据样本，分析e⁺e⁻→π⁰J/ψ过程，测量 dressed截面并提取分支比，同时考虑与ψ(3686)→π⁰J/ψ衰变的可能干涉效应。

Result: e⁺e⁻→π⁰J/ψ的dressed截面为(249±44±15) fb，ψ(3770)→π⁰J/ψ分支比为(2.08±0.36±0.21±0.25)×10⁻⁵。

Conclusion: 该分支比首次测定，显著低于四夸克模型预言，但与介子圈效应理论计算相符，为理解ψ(3770)衰变的同位旋破缺机制提供了关键实验信息。

Abstract: We report the first evidence for the hadronic transition $ψ(3770) \to π^0 J/ψ$ using a data sample of $20.3~\mathrm{fb}^{-1}$ collected at $\sqrt{s} = 3.773$~GeV with the BESIII detector. The $e^+ e^- \to π^{0}J/ψ$ process is observed with a statistical significance of $6.1σ$, while the significance specifically attributed to $ψ(3770) \to π^{0}J/ψ$ is $4.5σ$. We measure the dressed cross section for $e^+e^- \to π^0 J/ψ$ to be $(249 \pm 44 \pm 15)~\text{fb}$ and determine the branching fraction $\mathcal{B}(ψ(3770) \to π^0 J/ψ) = (2.08 \pm 0.36 \pm 0.21\pm 0.25) \times 10^{-5}$, where the first uncertainty is statistical, the second is systematic, and the third due to a possible interference with the $ψ(3686) \to π^{0}J/ψ$ decay. This is the first determination of this branching fraction. It lies significantly below tetraquark model predictions but aligns with calculations that incorporate meson loop effects, providing crucial insight into the isospin-violating nature of $ψ(3770)$ decays.

</details>


### [122] [Determining Neutrino Mass Ordering with NOvA and Upcoming JUNO Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14121)
*NOvA Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文研究了JUNO实验对反应堆中微子参数 |Δm²₃₂| 的精确测量如何提升NOvA实验对中微子质量顺序的判定能力。结果表明，在JUNO未来五年内可能的测量范围内，NOvA有望以3σ置信度确认正常质量顺序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NOvA实验利用十年数据结合反应堆实验外部约束已报告了质量顺序判定的显著性，而JUNO实验即将提供更精确的 |Δm²₃₂| 约束。本文旨在探索JUNO的预期测量对NOvA确定中微子质量顺序的潜在影响。

Method: 通过将JUNO对 |Δm²₃₂| 的预期精确测量作为外部约束，结合NOvA的十年数据，进行联合分析，评估不同JUNO测量结果下NOvA对正常质量顺序的证据强度。

Result: 在JUNO未来五年内一系列可能的测量范围内，NOvA能够以3σ显著性获得正常质量顺序的证据。

Conclusion: JUNO对 |Δm²₃₂| 的精确测量将显著增强NOvA对中微子质量顺序的判定能力，有望在五年内实现3σ水平的正常顺序确认。

Abstract: NOvA has reported a significance of mass ordering determination using ten years of data together with external constraints from reactor-based experiments. The JUNO collaboration is poised to provide a more precise reactor-based constraint on $|Δm^2_{32}|$. In this Letter, we explore the potential impact of this anticipated measurement on the determination of the neutrino mass ordering by NOvA. We find that $3σ$ evidence of the normal ordering is achievable over a range of plausible JUNO measurements within the next five years.

</details>


### [123] [A Novel Approach to Short Baseline Oscillation Searches Using Neutrino Tagging with nuSCOPE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14263)
*Adrien Blanchet,César Jesús-Valls,Animesh Chatterjee,Stephen Dolan,Pierre Granger,Laura Munteanu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文首次研究报告基于标记中微子束流的短基线振荡搜索，以CERN拟议的nuSCOPE设施为基准，展示了标记中微子束流在逐事例精度上测定中微子味、能量和传播距离的能力，为寻找非标准中微子振荡提供了新的实验方法。研究评估了对惰性中微子诱导的ν_μ消失、ν_μ→ν_e出现和ν_e消失通道的灵敏度，证明该实验能同时探测多种味跃迁及出现与消失模式，灵敏度覆盖多个量级的质量平方分裂，并显著降低对通量预测的依赖，可探测现有异常所启发的参数空间并扩展到此前未探索的区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索标记中微子束流这一新型实验技术用于短基线中微子振荡搜索的可行性，克服传统中微子振荡实验对通量预测的高度依赖，并覆盖更广的参数空间以检验现有异常现象及探索新物理。

Method: 以CERN拟议的nuSCOPE设施为基准，利用标记中微子束流技术（逐事例精确测定中微子味、能量和传播距离），对ν_μ消失、ν_μ→ν_e出现和ν_e消失三个通道进行惰性中微子诱导振荡的灵敏度分析。

Result: 标记束流能够实现多个量级质量平方分裂的灵敏度提升，大幅降低对中微子通量预测的依赖，且nuSCOPE可探测现有异常启发的广泛参数空间并拓展至此前未探索的区域。

Conclusion: 标记中微子束流技术在精密振荡物理方面具有强大潜力，为短基线中微子振荡搜索提供了全新的实验范式。

Abstract: We present the first study of short-baseline neutrino oscillation searches using a tagged neutrino beamline, taking the proposed nuSCOPE facility at CERN as a benchmark. In this Letter we demonstrate that tagged neutrino beams, where the neutrino flavor, energy, and propagation distance are determined with exceptional event-by-event precision, provide a new experimental approach to search for non-standard neutrino oscillations. We evaluate the sensitivity to sterile-neutrino-induced oscillations in the $ν_μ$ disappearance, $ν_μ\rightarrow ν_e$ appearance, and $ν_e$ disappearance channels, demonstrating the ability to probe multiple flavor transitions and both appearance and disappearance modes within a single experiment. Our results show that tagged beams enable sensitivity improvements to mass-squared splittings spanning several orders of magnitude while substantially reducing the dependence on neutrino flux predictions that limits conventional searches. We find that nuSCOPE can probe a broad region of parameter space motivated by existing anomalies and extend coverage into previously unexplored territory, demonstrating the strong potential of tagged neutrino beams for precision oscillation physics.

</details>


### [124] [Search for dark photons from Higgs boson decays in the gluon-gluon fusion channel in proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}=13.6$ TeV with the ATLAS detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14362)
*ATLAS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 利用135 fb⁻¹的ATLAS Run 3数据，通过光子加缺失横动量的末态，搜索希格斯玻色子半不可见衰变H→γγ_d，采用2023年新触发的低阈值(光子pT>50 GeV，缺失pT>70 GeV)和横质量mT>80 GeV选择，结合BDT修正顶点重建误差，未发现显著超出，观测(期望)上限为1.4%(1.2%)，联合Run 2结果改进至0.9%(0.9%)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找希格斯玻色子衰变为标准模型光子和暗光子(γ_d)的半不可见衰变过程，检验超出标准模型的新物理信号。

Method: 利用2023年部署的新型低阈值触发(光子pT≥50 GeV，缺失pT≥70 GeV，mT>80 GeV)；采用BDT鉴别误重建的主顶点事件以修正缺失动量计算；数据驱动方法估计喷流和电子误判为光子的本底；含真实光子的本底由MC模拟生成并用缪子控制区数据归一化；对mT分布做分箱最大似然拟合。

Result: 未观测到显著超出标准模型预期，观测(期望)分支比上限为1.4%(1.2%)@95%CL；联合Run 2结果将上限改进至0.9%(0.9%)。

Conclusion: 未发现H→γγ_d信号，给出了目前最严格的分支比上限，为暗光子与希格斯玻色子的耦合强度提供了新的实验约束。

Abstract: This paper presents a search for semi-visible Higgs boson decays in final states with a photon ($γ$) and missing transverse momentum $p_T^{miss}$. The analysis is optimised for the gluon-gluon fusion production channel and exploits a novel trigger deployed in 2023, with reduced thresholds down to 50 GeV on the photon transverse momentum ($p_T^γ$) and 70 GeV on the $p_T^{miss}$, in combination with a selection on the transverse mass of the $p_T^{miss}$-$γ$ system ($m_T$) above 80 GeV. The results are interpreted in terms of Higgs boson decays into a $γ$ and a dark photon $γ_d$ ($H \to γγ_d$). The analysis is performed using 135 fb$^{-1}$ of Run 3 data collected by ATLAS in 2023 and 2024. A boosted decision tree is used to identify events with misreconstructed primary vertices, which can affect the $p_T^{miss}$ calculation. Data-driven methods are employed to estimate the backgrounds from jets and electrons misidentified as photons, while the backgrounds with genuine photons are taken from Monte Carlo simulations, normalised to data in control regions with muons. A binned maximum-likelihood fit to the $m_T$ distribution is performed to evaluate the compatibility of the observed data with the Standard Model expectation. No significant excess is observed over the Standard Model prediction, and an observed (expected) upper limit on the branching ratio of $H \to γγ_d$ of 1.4% (1.2%) is obtained at 95% confidence level. Combining this result with previous Run 2 searches improves the observed (expected) upper limit to 0.9% (0.9%).

</details>


### [125] [Machine-learned particle flow as a foundation model for collider physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14373)
*Farouk Mokhtar,Joosep Pata,Michael Kagan,Javier Duarte*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出将粒子碰撞事件重建过程中的机器学习模型（MLPF）作为基础模型，其学到的潜在表示可直接迁移至下游物理分析任务（喷注味道识别、喷注能量回归、缺失动量回归），仅用线性层即可在参数减少约35倍的情况下达到或超越现有基线性能，为实现从探测器数据到物理分析的端到端流水线迈出了实质性一步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统粒子碰撞事件重建流程中各步骤模块化、相互割裂，缺乏连接底层探测器数据与高层分析任务的共享表示，导致信息在逐级传递中可能丢失或冗余。

Method: 将用于粒子流重建的机器学习模型（MLPF）重新用于下游分析任务：提取重建过程中每个粒子的潜在表示作为额外输入特征，分别训练喷注味道识别、喷注能量回归和缺失动量回归任务；对比仅使用运动学特征、完整MLPF模型以及仅用潜在表示的线性层等多种设置。

Result: 潜在表示显著提升了各项下游任务的性能；仅用潜在表示的单个线性层在缺失动量回归任务上以约35倍更少的参数超越了现有最优基线架构；在其他任务上也达到了具有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 重建过程中学到的潜在表示编码了下游物理分析所需的关键物理信息，证明了MLPF可作为基础模型，为构建从探测器数据到物理分析的端到端流水线提供了具体路径。

Abstract: The workflow from particle collision to physics analysis passes through a series of reconstruction steps that are traditionally modular and disconnected, with no shared representation linking low-level detector data to high-level analysis tasks. We show that casting event reconstruction as a machine learning problem naturally produces such a shared representation. We repurpose a machine learning model trained for particle-flow reconstruction (MLPF) to perform three distinct analysis tasks: jet flavor identification, jet energy regression, and missing momentum regression. By appending the per-particle latent representations learned during reconstruction as additional input features, we substantially improve over baselines that use kinematic features alone. We further demonstrate that a single linear layer trained using only the latent representations achieves competitive performance against state-of-the-art baseline architectures, and outperforms the baseline for missing momentum regression with approximately 35 times fewer parameters. These results demonstrate that the latent representations learned during reconstruction encode essential physics information needed for downstream analysis, establishing MLPF as a foundation model and offering a concrete step toward an end-to-end pipeline from detector data to physics analysis.

</details>

TL;DR: SND@LHC实验利用LHC Run 3期间采集的68.6 fb⁻¹质子-质子对撞数据，测量了钨靶上的缪子中微子带电电流相互作用截面，在228 GeV中位能量下得到σ(ν_μ+反ν_μ) = (37⁺²⁴₋₁₂)×10⁻³⁵ cm²，信号强度̂μ = 1.09⁺⁰·⁷²₋₀·₃₇。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在LHC上首次利用SND@LHC实验的电子探测器测量缪子中微子在钨靶上的带电电流相互作用截面，验证标准模型预言并为中微子物理提供实验数据。

Method: 使用SND@LHC实验电子探测器，基于LHC Run 3期间√s=13.6 TeV质子-质子对撞数据（积分亮度68.6 fb⁻¹），选取31个ν_μ CC候选事件（预期背景5.0±1.1，信号期望24⁺¹⁰₋₉），利用专用束流测试标定数据进行量能器测量。

Result: 信号强度̂μ=1.09⁺⁰·⁷²₋₀·₃₇，缪子中微子与反中微子CC总截面σ(ν_μ+反ν_μ) = (37⁺²⁴₋₁₂)×10⁻³⁵ cm²（中位能量228 GeV），并实现了强子能量的量能器测量。

Conclusion: 测量结果与标准模型预期一致，展示了SND@LHC实验在LHC环境下进行中微子截面测量的能力，为后续更高精度测量奠定了基础。

Abstract: We report a measurement of the muon neutrino charged-current (CC) interaction cross section on tungsten using the electronic detectors of the SND@LHC experiment at the CERN Large Hadron Collider. The analysis uses proton--proton collision data at a centre-of-mass energy of $\sqrt{s} = 13.6$ TeV, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of $68.6 ~\text{fb}^{-1}$ collected during LHC Run 3 in 2022 and 2023. A total of 31 $ν_μ$ CC candidates are selected against an expected background of $5.0 \pm 1.1$ events, consistent with a signal expectation of $24^{+10}_{-9}$ events. The signal strength is measured to be $\hatμ = 1.09^{+0.72}_{-0.37}$, and the combined muon neutrino and anti-neutrino CC cross section on tungsten is determined to be $σ(ν_μ+ \barν_μ) = (37^{+24}_{-12})\times 10^{-35}~\text{cm}^2$ at a median energy of $228$ GeV. In addition, a calorimetric measurement of the hadronic energies of the neutrino candidate events is performed, making use of calibration data from dedicated test-beam campaigns.

</details>
